=== PROMPT VARIANT ===
r1

=== PROMPT ===
You will be given a competitive programming problem.
On a certain planet, there are N aliens, all of whom are minors.
The i-th alien currently has A_i stones, and will become an adult exactly i years later.
When someone becomes an adult on this planet, every adult who has at least one stone gives exactly one stone as a congratulatory gift to the alien who has just become an adult.
Find how many stones each alien will have after N years.
Assume that no new aliens will be born in the future.

Input

The input is given from Standard Input in the following format:
N
A_1 A_2 \ldots A_N

Output

Let B_i be the number of stones owned by the i-th alien after N years. Print B_1, B_2, \ldots, B_N in this order, separated by spaces.

Constraints


- 1 \leq N \leq 5 \times 10^5
- 0 \leq A_i \leq 5 \times 10^5
- All input values are integers.

Sample Input 1

4
5 0 9 3

Sample Output 1

2 0 10 5

Let C_i be the number of stones that the i-th alien has at a given time.
Initially, (C_1, C_2, C_3, C_4) = (5, 0, 9, 3).
After 1 year, (C_1, C_2, C_3, C_4) = (5, 0, 9, 3).
After 2 years, (C_1, C_2, C_3, C_4) = (4, 1, 9, 3).
After 3 years, (C_1, C_2, C_3, C_4) = (3, 0, 11, 3).
After 4 years, (C_1, C_2, C_3, C_4) = (2, 0, 10, 5).

Sample Input 2

5
4 6 7 2 5

Sample Output 2

0 4 7 4 9

Sample Input 3

10
2 9 1 2 0 4 6 7 1 5

Sample Output 3

0 2 0 0 0 4 7 10 4 10

Reason in a commit-implement-recheck way.

A productive rhythm for this task usually looks like this:
- Isolate the decisive constraints, the target complexity, and at most one or two approaches that are still truly plausible.
- If two candidates remain, compare them only on the decisive constraint and commit quickly to the stronger one instead of keeping a wide search open.
- Before coding, keep only a tiny implementation ledger for the chosen plan: main invariant, key state or transition, and the one or two edge cases most likely to fail.
- Move into code as soon as that ledger is clear. After coding starts, keep reasoning attached to the current draft rather than reopening the high-level search.
- After the first complete draft, do one short implementation-check-adjust-check loop on the same draft: test the key edge case, patch locally if needed, and recheck.
- Reopen the main design only if a concrete counterexample breaks the core invariant. Otherwise prefer the local patch and keep closing.
- Once the current draft, invariant, and short recheck still agree, finalize the solution.

Try to avoid patterns like these:
- spending too long comparing nearby plans before committing to one workable approach;
- returning from code to broad algorithm discussion without a concrete contradiction;
- turning one local doubt into a full restart when the current draft only needs a small patch.

Output only the final Python code enclosed in a code block.

Let's formulate final code:

import sys
def main():
    data = list(map(int, sys.stdin.buffer.read().split()))
    if not data: return
    N = data[0]
    A = data[1:1+N]
    diff = [0]*(N+2)  # indices 0..N+1
    active = 0
    ans = []
    for i, a in enumerate(A, start=1):
        active += diff[i]
        s = a + active
        b = s - (N - i)
        if b < 0:
            b = 0
        ans.append(str(b))
        if i < N and s > 0:
            diff[i+1] += 1
            t = s + i
            if t + 1 <= N:
                diff[t+1] -= 1
    print(' '.join(ans))
if __name__ == "__main__": main()

Now, let's think about possible issue with "all of whom are minors" and "will become an adult exactly i years later": If alien i becomes adult exactly i years later, after N years alien N just became adult. We process at year i. Good.

Now, let's consider if there is a closed form maybe using difference array of "deadline" T_i. Our algorithm is essentially computing S_i = A_i + active, where active is number of previous deadlines >= i. We maintain active with diff events. This is O(N). Good.

Let's ensure diff array update order: We compute s using active after applying diff[i]. Then update diff for future. If we updated diff before computing s, would include self incorrectly. We do after. Good.

Now, let's consider if active += diff[i] should happen before or after computing B_i? At time i, before event, previous adults have already gone through years up to i-1. The diff events at i represent removals of previous aliens whose T_j = i-1 (they became zero after giving at time i-1? Let's check: If T_j = i-1, then at time i-1 before event, alien j was positive and gave one, after event its count becomes S_j - (i-1 - j) -1? Let's derive. Condition T_j = S_j+j = i-1. At time i-1 before event, count = S_j - ((i-1)-1 - j)? Wait just before time k, count = S_j - (k-1 - j) if positive. At time k=i-1, before event count = S_j - (i-2 - j). It gives one, after event count = S_j - (i-1 - j) = T_j - (i-1) = 0. So at time i, it is zero and should not contribute. Removal at time i (T_j+1 = i) is correct. If T_j = i, at time i before event count = S_j - (i-1-j) = T_j - (i-1) =1, positive, gives one, after event zero. It should contribute to P_i. Removal at time i+1. Good. Code:
import sys

def main():
    data = list(map(int, sys.stdin.buffer.read().split()))
    if not data:
        return
    N = data[0]
    diff = [0] * (N + 2)
    active = 0
    ans = []
    for i in range(1, N + 1):
        active += diff[i]
        s = data[i] + active
        b = s - (N - i)
        if b < 0:
            b = 0
        ans.append(str(b))
        if i < N and s > 0:
            diff[i + 1] += 1
            t = s + i
            if t + 1 <= N:
                diff[t + 1] -= 1
    sys.stdout.write(" ".join(ans))

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

Let's maybe derive recurrence from another perspective to ensure S_i = A_i + active_count, where active_count = number of previous aliens with positive stones just before i. We used T_j = S_j + j. Let's prove T_j deadline:
Alien j after its maturation has S_j stones. At the start of year k (k>j), before the ceremony, it has gone through k-1-j ceremonies where it may have given. Since it gives only if positive, its count is max(S_j - (k-1-j), 0). It is positive iff S_j - (k-1-j) > 0 iff S_j + j > k-1 iff S_j + j >= k. Thus T_j = S_j + j. Good.

Now, difference array events: For alien j with S_j>0, it is active for k = j+1, ..., T_j. Add +1 at j+1, -1 at T_j+1. If T_j>N, no removal needed. If S_j=0, T_j=j, no active times. Good.

Now, final B_i: Alien i after its maturation has S_i. It then participates in ceremonies i+1,...,N, at most N-i times, giving one if positive. Its final count is max(S_i - (N-i), 0). Good.

Let's ensure code has no syntax errors:
import sys

def main():
    data = list(map(int, sys.stdin.buffer.read().split()))
    if not data:
        return
    N = data[0]
    diff = [0] * (N + 2)
    active = 0
    ans = []
    for i in range(1, N + 1):
        active += diff[i]
        s = data[i] + active
        b = s - (N - i)
        if b < 0:
            b = 0
        ans.append(str(b))
        if i < N and s > 0:
            diff[i + 1] += 1
            t = s + i
            if t + 1 <= N:
                diff[t + 1] -= 1
    sys.stdout.write(" ".join(ans))

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

Let's maybe implement memory-conscious version with line reading and StringIO to be safe:
import sys
from io import StringIO

def main():
    input = sys.stdin.buffer.readline
    N = int(input())
    A = list(map(int, input().split()))
    diff = [0] * (N + 2)
    active = 0
    out = StringIO()
    for i, a in enumerate(A, 1):
        active += diff[i]
        s = a + active
        b = s - (N - i)
        if b < 0:
            b = 0
        if i > 1:
            out.write(' ')
        out.write(str(b))
        if i < N and s > 0:
            diff[i + 1] += 1
            t = s + i
            if t + 1 <= N:
                diff[t + 1] -= 1
    sys.stdout.write(out.getvalue())

But StringIO with 500k writes maybe okay. However, `out.write(' ')` and `out.write(str(b))` 1e6 method calls, still okay for 5e5. Could be slower than join but acceptable. To be safe on time, list join is faster. Memory okay. I'd use list join.

Could use `ans = [None] * N` and fill strings, then join. Same memory. `ans.append` okay.

Now, let's decide final code. I think code with data list and ans list is fine. But to be more memory-conscious and still simple, I can use:
import sys

def main():
    data = list(map(int, sys.stdin.buffer.read().split()))
    N = data[0]
    diff = [0] * (N + 2)
    active = 0
    ans = []
    for i in range(1, N + 1):
        active += diff[i]
        s = data[i] + active
        b = s - (N - i)
        if b < 0:
            b = 0
        ans.append(str(b))
        if i < N and s > 0:
            diff[i + 1] += 1
            t = s + i
            if t + 1 <= N:
                diff[t + 1] -= 1
    sys.stdout.write(" ".join(ans))

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

Let's maybe do a more complex random brute force by hand for N=4, A=[0,1,0,2].
Process:
Initial (0,1,0,2)
t1 alien1 adult, no previous, C1=0.
t2 alien2 adult, previous positive none? alien1=0, so C2=1 (A2=1). C=(0,1,0,2)
t3 alien3 adult, previous positive alien2=1 gives to alien3 => C=(0,0,1,2)
t4 alien4 adult, previous positive alien3=1 gives to alien4; alien4 A4=2 self no net => C=(0,0,0,3)
Final (0,0,0,3).
Algorithm:
i1 s0 b0 no event.
i2 active0 s1 b=max(1-2,0)=0; diff3+1 t3? S2=1 T=3, t+1=4<=N diff4-1.
i3 active += diff3=1 s=0+1=1 b=max(1-1,0)=0; diff4+1 t4? S3=1 T=4, t+1=5>N skip.
i4 active += diff4 (-1+1=0) =>1 s=2+1=3 b3. Output 0 0 0 3. Good.

N=4, A=[1,2,0,0].
Process:
t1 C1=1
t2 alien2 adult: alien1 gives to alien2; alien2 A2=2 self no net => C=(0,3,0,0)
t3 alien3 adult: alien2 gives to alien3 => C=(0,2,1,0)
t4 alien4 adult: alien2 gives to alien4, alien3 gives to alien4 => C=(0,1,0,2)
Final (0,1,0,2).
Algorithm:
i1 s1 b=max(1-3,0)=0; diff2+1 t2? T=2, t+1=3<=N diff3-1.
i2 active1 s=2+1=3 b=max(3-2,0)=1; diff3+1 t5? S2=3 T=5, skip.
i3 active += diff3 (-1+1=0) =>1 s=0+1=1 b=max(1-1,0)=0; diff4+1 t4? S3=1 T=4, skip.
i4 active += diff4=1 =>2 s=0+2=2 b2. Output 0 1 0 2. Good.

=== ANSWER ===
with final code.

```python
import sys

def main():
    data = list(map(int, sys.stdin.buffer.read().split()))
    if not data:
        return

    N = data[0]
    diff = [0] * (N + 2)
    active = 0
    ans = []

    for i in range(1, N + 1):
        active += diff[i]

        s = data[i] + active
        b = s - (N - i)
        if b < 0:
            b = 0
        ans.append(str(b))

        if i < N and s > 0:
            diff[i + 1] += 1
            t = s + i
            if t + 1 <= N:
                diff[t + 1] -= 1

    sys.stdout.write(" ".join(ans))

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```
